RESERVE BANK OF INDIA 
DEPARTMENT OF FINANCIAL COMPANIES 
CENTRAL OFFICE 
Calcutta-700 001, the 19th April 1994 

No. DFC(COC) No. 73. ED(S)-94.—-The Reserve Bank of 
India, having considered It necessary In the public Interest 
and being satisfied that, for the purpose of enabling the Bank 
to regulate the credit system to the advantage of the coun¬ 
try, it is necessary to amend the Non-Banking Financial 
Companies (Reserve Bank") Directions, 1977, hereby, In 
exercise of the powers conferred by Sections 45J, 45K and 
45L of the Reserve Bank of India Act, 1934 (2 of 1934) and 
of all the powers enabling it in this behalf directs that the 
said Directions contained in Notification No. DNBC. 38/ 
DG(H)-77 dated the 20th June 1977, with immediate effect, 
stands amended in the following manner, namely, 

1. In paragraph 3, in clause (il), the words and figures 
“clause (b ii) of Section 2 of the Reserve Bank of India Act, 
1934 (2 of 1934)" shall be substituted by the following— 

“Section 5(cci) of the Banking Regulation Act, 1949 (10 
of 1949)". 

2. In paragraph 11, the Explanation shall be deleted. 

3. The format of the FIRST SCHEDULE shall be substi¬ 
tuted by the one appended. 

4. Id SECOND SCHEDULE the address of Bombay 
Regional Office shall be substituted by the following 

“Bombay Regional Office, 

Garment House, 4th floor. 

Dr. Annie Besant Road, Worli, 

Bombay 400 018.” 

W. S. SARAF 
Executive Director 


No. DFC(COC) No. 74 ED(S)94.—The Reserve Bank of 
India, having considered it necessary in the public interest 
and being satisfied that, for the purpose of enabling the Bank 
to regulate the credit system to the advantage of the country, 
it is necessary to amend the Miscellaneous Non-Banking 
Companies (Reserve Bank) Directions, 1977 hereby, in exer¬ 
cise of the powers conferred by sections 451, 45K and 45L 
of the Reserve Bank of India Act, 1934 (2 of 1934) and of 
all the powciv enabling it in this behalf, directs that the said 
Direction contained in Notification No. DNBC. 39/DG(H)- 
77 dated the 20th June 1977, with immediate effect, stands 
amended in the following manner, namely, 

1. In paragraph 4, in clause (iii), the words and figures 
‘clause (b ii) of Section 2 of the Reserve Bank of 
India Act, 1934 (2 of 1934)” shall be substituted by 
tbs following;— 

“Section 5 (cci) of the Banking Regulation Act, 1949 
(10 cf 1949)." 


2. In paragraph 9B, the Explanation shall be deleted. 

3. The format of the FIRST SCHEDULE shall be sub¬ 
stituted by the one appended. 

4. In the SECOND SCHEDULE the address of Bombay 
Regional Office shall be substituted by the follow¬ 
ing 

“Bombay Regional Office, 

Garment House, 

4th floor, 

Dr. Annie Besant Road, 

Worli, 

Bombay-400 018." 


W. S. SARAF 
Executive Director

Part III— Sec. 4] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, JUNE 18, 1994 (JYAISTHA 28, 1916) 


INSTRUCTIONS FOR FILLING IN THF. RHTURN 

1. The return should he submitted by a non-banking linan- 
ciiit company covered by Notification No. DNBC 38/DG 
(Hl-77 dated Ihe 70th June 1077 and by a miscellaneous 
non-banking company covered by Notification No. DNBC 
39/'DG(H)-77 dated the 20th June 1977 once a year before 
Ihe 30lh June with relerence to its position as on the 31st 
March irrespective of the date of closing of the financial 
year of the company concerned. 

2. The submission of the return should not be delayed for 

any reason such as the finalisation./completion oE the audit 
of the annual accounts. The compilation of the return 
should be on the basis of the figures available in the books 
of account of the company. , . 

3. The number of accounts should be given in actual figures 
while the amounts of deposits should be in thousands of 
rupees. Amount should be rounded off to the nearest 
thousand. For example an amount of Rs. 4.360 should be 
shown as 5 and not as 4,6 or 5.000. Similarly art amount of 
Rs. 61.495 is to be shown as 61 and not as 61.4 or 61,000. 

4. The period wise classification of deposits should he made 
against the various heads under item No. 6 of Part I of the 
leturn according to the periods for which they have been 
originally received/last renewed and not according to the 
periods they have to run as from the 31st March i.c. the date 
of return. 

5. “Free Reserves” mentioned under item 1 of Part 3 shall 
include the balance in the share premium account, capital and 
debenture redemption reserves and any other reserve shown or 


published in the balance sheet and created through an alloca¬ 
tion oE profits hut not being : 

(i) a resetve created for repayment of any future liability 
or lor depreciation of assets or for bad debts or 

i iil a reserve crca'ed by revaluation of Ihe assets of the 
company. 

6. A list containing Ihe names and the designations of Ihe 
principal officers as also the names and addresses of the direc¬ 
tors of the company should ho attached in case it has not so 
far been sent to the Reserve Bank as required by paragraph 
14(2) of the notification No. DNBC. 38/DGfH)-77 or by 
paragraph 11(2) of Notification No. DNBC. 39/DG(H)-77 
dated 20lh lune, 1977 as the case may be and any change in 
Ihe list should also be intimated to the office of the Reserve 
Bank to which this return is submitted. 

7. The return should be signed by Manager (as defined in 
Section 2 of the Companies Act 1956) and if there is no such 
Manager, by Managing Director or any official of the company 
who has been duly authorised by the Board of directors and 
whose specimen signature has been furnished to the Reserve 
Bank for the purpose. In case the specimen signature has 
not been furnished in the prescribed card, the return must be 
signed bv the authorised official and his specimen signature 
furnished separately. 

K. In case there is nothing to report in any part of return, 
it should be marked ‘Nil 1 and the Manager’s/Managing Direc- 
lor’.yAuthorised officials Certificate appended to the return 
should be difly signed.

INSTRUCTIONS FOR FILLING IN THE RETURN 

1. The return should be submitted by a Residuary Non- 
Fankino Company covered by Notification No, DFC. 55/DG 
(OJ-S7 dated the 15th May 1987 oiux a year before the 30th 
lune with reference to its position as on the 31st March irres 
pretive ol' the date of clodnp of the financial year of the 
company concerned. 

2. The submission of the return should not be delayed for 
any reason such as the finalisation/completion of the audit of 
the annual account*. The compilation of the return should 
be on the basis of the figures available in the books of account 
of the company. 

3. The number of accounts should be given in actual figures 
while the amounts of deposits should be given in thousands 
of rupees. Amount should be rounded off to the nearest 
thousand. For example an amount of Rs. 4,560 should be 
shown as 5 and not as 4,6 or 5000. Similarly an amount of 
Rs, 61.495 is to be shown as 61 and not as 61.4 or 61,000. 

4. The period-wise classification of deposits should be made 
aglainst the various heads under item No. 2 of Part 1 of the 
return according to the periods for which they have been ori¬ 


ginally received/last. renewed and not according to the period 
they "have to run as from the 31st March i.e. the date of 

the return 

5. A list containing the names and the designations of the- 
principal officers as also the names and addresses of the direc¬ 
tors of the company should be attached in case it has not so 
far been ^ent to Rercrve Bank as required by paragraph 14(2) 
of the Notification No. DFC. 53/DG(0)-87 dated the 15th 
May 1987 and any change in the list should also be intimated 
to the office of the Reserve Bank to which this return is sub¬ 
mitted. 

6. The return should be signed by the Manager (a* defi¬ 
ned in Section 2 of the Companies Act. 1956) and If there 
is no such Manager, by the, Managing Director or any offi¬ 
cial of the company who has been duly authorised by the 
Board of Directors and whose specimen signature has been^ 
furnished to the Reserve Bank for the purpose. In case the 
specimen signature has not been furnished in the prescribed 
card, the return may be signed by the authorised official and 
his specimen signature furnished separately. 

7. In case there is nothing to report in any part of the re¬ 
turn, it should be marked *1411’ and the Manager’s /Managing 
Director’s Authorised Official’s ertificate appended to the 
return should be duly signed.

No 2/1959/DU /Bxemp/89/Pt. 1/1672 — WHEREAS 
the employers of the establishments mentioned in Sche¬ 
dule-1 (hereinafter referred to us the laid establishment) have 
applied for exemption under Sub-section 2 (A) of Section 17 
of the Employees’ Provident Funds and Miscellaneous Pro¬ 
visions Act, 1952 (19 of 1952) (hereinafter referred to as 
the said Act.) 

AND WHEREAS, I.B.N. SOM, Central Provident Fund 
Commissioner, is satisfied that the employees of the said 
establishment are, without making any separate contribution 
or payment of premium in enjoyment of benefits under the 
Ground Insurance Scheme of the l ife Insurance Corpora¬ 
tion of India in the nature of Life Insurance which arc more 
favourable to such employees than the benefits admissible 
under the Employees’ Deposit Linked Insurance Scheme, 
1976 (hereinafter referred to as the said Scheme). 

NOW, THEREFORE, in exercise of the powers conferred 
by Sub-section 2(A) of Section 17 of the said Act is continua¬ 
tion of the Government of India in the Ministry of Labour/ 
C\ P, F. C. Notification No. and date shown against the 
name of each of the establishment and subject to the condi¬ 
tion-,' specified in Schedule-IT annexed hereto, I, B. N. SOM. 
hereby exempt each said establishment from the operation 
of all tlie provisions of the said scheme for a further period 
of 3 years as indicated in attached Schedule against their 
names.

Part III— Sec. 4] THB GAZETTE OF INDIA, JUNE 18, 1994 (JYAISTHA 28, 1916) 3205 


SCHEDULE-11 


1. The employer in relation to each of the said establish¬ 
ments (hereinafter referred to as the employer) shall submit 
such returns to the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner 
concerned and maintain such accounts and provide such faci¬ 
lities for inspection, as the Central Provident Fund Commis¬ 
sioner mav direct from time to time. 

2. The employer shall pav such inspection charges as the 
Central Government may. from time to time, direct under 
Clause (a) of Sub-Section 3 (A) of Section 17 of the said 
Act. within 15 days from the close of every month. 


3. All expenses involved in the administration of the Group 
Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, sub¬ 
mission of returns, payment of insurance premia, transfer 
of accounts, payment of inspection charge etc. shall be borne 
by the employer. 

4. The employer shall display on the Notice Board of the 
establishment, a copy of the rales of the Group Insurance 
Scheme as approved by the Central Government /Central 
Provident Fund Commissioner as and when amended, alonp- 
with translation ot the salient features thereof in the language 
of the majority of the employees. 

5. Whereas an employee, who is already a member of the 
Employees’ Piovident Fund or the Provident Fund of an 
establishment exempted under the said Act. is employed in 
his establishment the emplover shall immediately admit him 
as a member of the Group Insurance Scheme and pay neces¬ 
sary Premium in respect of him to the Life Insurance Corpo¬ 
ration of India. 

6. The cmnloyer shall arrange to enhance the benefits 
available to the employees under the Group Insurance 
Scheme appropriately if the benefits available to the em¬ 
ployees under the said Scheme are enhanced so that the 
benefits available under the Group Insurance Scheme are 
more favourable to the employees than the benefits admis¬ 
sible under the said Scheme. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group 
Insurance Scheme, if on the death of an employee the 
amounts payable under the scheme be less than the amount 
that would be payable had the employee been covered under 
the said Scheme, the employer shall pav the difference to 
the nominee(s) /legal helrfs) of the employee ns compensa¬ 
tion. 

8. No amendment of the provisions of the Group Insu¬ 
rance Scheme shall be made without the prior approval of 
the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner concerned and 
where anv amendment is likely to affect adversely the 
Interest of the employees, the Regional Provident Fund Com¬ 
missioner shall before giving his approval, gives a reasonable 
opportunity to the employees to explain their point of view. 


9. Where for any reason, the employees of the said esta¬ 
blishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India us already 
adopted by the said establishment, or the benefits to the 
employees under this scheme are reduced in any manner 
the exemption shall he liable to be cancelled. 


10. Where for any reason, (he employer fails to pay the 
premium etc. within the due date, as Jived by the Life Insu¬ 
rance Corporation of India, and the poliev is. allowed to lapse, 
the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

11. In case of default, if any made by the employer in pay¬ 
ment of premium the responsibility for payment of assurance 
benefits to the nominee!s)/legal heir(s) of deceased member, 
who would have been covered under the said scheme bill for 
grant of this exemption, shall be that of the employer. 

12. Upon the death of the member covered under the 
Group Insurance Scheme the Life Insurance Corporation of 
India shall ensure prompt payment of the sum assured to the 
nomineefsl/leital heir(s) of Ihe deceased member entitled 
for it and in any case within one month from tile receipt of 
claims complete in all respects.

SCHEDULE-II 

1. The employer in relation to each of the said establish¬ 
ment (hereinafter referred to as the employer) shall submit 
such returns to the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner 
concerned and maintain such accounts and provide such faci¬ 
lities for inspection, as the Central Provident Fund Commis¬ 
sioner may direct from time to time. 

2. The employer shall pay such inspection charges as the 
Central Government, may, from time to time, direct under 
clause (a) of sub-Section (3A) of Section 17 of the said Act, 
within 15 days from the close of every month. 

3. All expenses involved in the administration of the Group 
Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, sub¬ 
mission of returns, payment of insurance premia, transfer 
of accounts, payment of inspection charges etc. shall be borne 
by the employer. 

4. The employer shall display on the Notice Board of the 
establishment, a copy of the rules of the Group Insurance 
Scheme as approved by the Central Government/Central 
Provident Fund Commissioner as and when amended, nlong- 
with translation of the salient features therof in the language 
of the majority of the employees. 

5. Whereas an emplovee, who is alreadv a member of the 
Employees’ Provident Fund or the Provident Fund of an 
establishment exempted under the said Act, is employed in 
his establishment the emplover shall immediately admit him 
as a member of the Grout* Insurance Scheme and pay neces¬ 
sary Premium in respect of him to the Life Insurance Corpo¬ 
ration of India. 

6 The employer shall arrange to enhance the benefits 
available to the employees under the Group Insurance 
Scheme appropriately if the benefits available to the era- 
nlovees under the said Scheme are enhanced so that the 
benefits available under the Group Insurance Scheme are 
more favourable to the employees than the benefits admis¬ 
sible under the said Scheme. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group 
Insurance Scheme, if on the death of an employee the 
amounts payable under the scheme be less than the amount 
that would be payable had the employee been covered under 
the said Scheme, the emnlover shall pay the difference to 
the nominee(s)/legal heirs) of the employee as compensa¬ 
tion. 

8. No amendment of the provisions of the Group Insu¬ 
rance Scheme shall be made without the prior approval of 
the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner concerned and 
where anv amendment is likely to effect adversely the 
interest of the employees, his approval, give a reasonable 
opportunity to the emplovee to explain their point of view. 


9. Where for any reason, the employees of the said esta¬ 
blishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as already 
adopted by the said establishment, or the benefits to the 
employees under this scheme are reduced in any manner, 
the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

10. Where for any reason, the employer faile to pay (he 
premium etc. within the due date, as fixed by the Life Insu¬ 
rance Corporation of India, and the policy is allowed to lapse, 
the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

11. In case of default, if any made by the employer in pay¬ 
ment of premium the responsibility for payment of assurance 
benefits to the nominee (s)/legal heir(s) of deceased member, 
who would have been covered under the said scheme but for 
grant of this exemption, shall be that of the employer. 

12. Upon the death of the member covered under the 
Group Insurance Scheme the Life Insurance Corporation of 
India shall ensure prompt payment of the sum assured to. the 
nominee(s)/Iegal heir(s) of the deceased member entitled 
for it and in any case within one month from the receipt of 
claims complete in all respects.

Central Provident Fund Commissioner

SCHEDULE II 

1. The employer in relation to cueh of the said establish¬ 
ments (hereafter refcircd to as the employer) shall submit 
such returns to the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner 
concerned and maintain such accounts and provide such luci- 
lities for inspection, as the Central Provident Fund Commis¬ 
sioner may direct from time to time. 

2. The employer shall pay such inspection charges as die 
Central Government may, from time to time, direct under 
Clause(a) of Sub-Section 2(A) of Setion 17 of the said Act, 
within 15 days front the close of every month. 

3. All expenses involved in the administration of the Group 
Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, sub¬ 
mission of returns, payment of insurance premia, transfer 
of accounts, payment of inspection charges etc, shall be borne 
by the employer. 

4. The employer shall display on the Notice Board of the 
establishment, a copy of the rules of the Group Insurance 
Scheme ns approved by the Central Govcrnment/Central 
Provident Fund Commissioner as and when amended, along- 
with translation of the salient features therof in tile langunge 
of the majority of the employees. 

5. Whereas an employee, who is already a member of the 
Employees’ Provident Fund or the Provident Fund of an 
establishment exempted under the said Act, is employed in 
his establishment the employer shall immediately admit him 
as a member of the Group insurance Scheme and pay neces¬ 
sary Premium in respect of him to the Life Insurance Corpo¬ 
ration of India. 

6. The employer shall arrange to enhance the benefits 
available to the employees under the Group Insurance 
.Scheme appropriately if the benefits available to the em¬ 
ployees under the said Scheme are enhanced so that the 
benefits available under the Group Insurance Scheme arc 
more favourable to the employees than the benefits admis¬ 
sible under the said Scheme. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group 
Insurance Scheme, if on the death of on employee the 
amounts payable under the scheme bo less than the amount 
that would be payable had the employee been covered under 
the said Scheme, the employor shall pay the difference to 
the nominee(s)/legal heir(s) of the employee ns compensa¬ 
tion. 

S. No amendment of the provisions of the Group Insu¬ 
rance Scheme shall be made without the prior approval of 
the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner concerned and 
where any amendment is likely to affect adversely the 
interest of the employers, his approval, gives a reasonable 
opportunity to the employees to explain their point of view. 

S CITED 


9. Where for any reason, the employees of the said establish¬ 
ment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as already 
adopted by the said establishment, or the benefits to the 
employees under this scheme are reduced in any manner 
the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

10. Where for uny reason, the employer fails to pay the 
premium etc. within the due date, as fixed by the Life Insu¬ 
rance Corporation of India, and the policy is allowed to lapse, 
the exemption shall bo liable to be cancelled, 

11. In case of default, if any made by the employer ini pay¬ 
ment of premium the responsibility for payment of assurance 
benefits to the nominee(s)/legal heir(s) of deceased member 
who would have been covered under the said scheme but for 
grant of this exemption, shall be that of the employer. 

12. Upon the death of the member covered under the 
Group Insurance Scheme this Life Insurance Corporation of 
India shall ensure prompt payment of the sum assured to the 
nomineo(s)/legal heir(s) of the deceased member entitled 
tor it and in any case within one month from the receipt of 
claims complete in all respects, 

B. N. SOM, 

Central Provident Fund Commissioner

SCHEDULED 


1. The employer in relation to each of the said establldi- 
ment (hereinafter referred to as the employer) shall submit 
such returns to the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner 
concerned and maintain such accounts and provide such faci¬ 
lities for inspection, as the Central Provident Fund Commis- 
scnior may direct from time to time. 

2. The employer shall pay such inspection charges as the 
Central Government may, from time to time, direct under 
clause (a) of subjection 2(A) of Section 17 of the said Act 
within 15 days from the close of every month. 

3. All expenses involved in the administration of the Group 
Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, sub¬ 
mission of returns, payment of insurance premia, transfer 
of accounts, payment of inspection charge etc. shall be borne 
by the employer. 

4. The employer shall display on the Notice Board of the 
establishment, copy of the rules of the Group Insurance 
Scheme as approved by the Central Government /Central 
Provident Fund Commissioner as and when amended, along- 
trilh translation of the salient features thereof in the language 
of the majority of the employees. 

5. Whereas an employee, who is already a member of the 
Employees’ Provident Fund or the Provident Fund of an 
establishment exempted under the said Act. is employed in 
his establishment the employer shall immediately admit him 
as a member of the Group Insurance Scheme and pay neces¬ 
sary Premium in respect of him to the life Insurance Corpo¬ 
ration of India. 

6. The employsr shall arrange to enhance the benefits 
available to the employee* under the Group Insurance Scheme 
appropriately if the benefits available to the employees under 
the said scheme are enhanced so that the benefits available 
Tinder the Group Insurance Scheme are more favourable to 
the employ*** than the benefit* admiteible under the said 
•chess*. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group Insu¬ 
rance Scheme, if on the death of an employee the amounts 
payable under the scheme be less than the amount that would 
be payable had the employee been covered under the said 
Scheme, the employer shall pay the difference to the 
nominee(s)/legal hcir(s) of the employee ai compensation. 

8. No amendment of the provisions of the Group Insurance 
Scheme shall be made without the prior approval of the 
Regional Provident Fund Commissioner concerned and where 
any amendment is likely to affect adversely the interest of 
employee*, the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner shall 
before giving his approval, gives a reasonable opportunity 
to the employees to explain their point of view. 


9. Where for any reason, the employees of the said esta¬ 
blishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as already 
adopted by the said establishment, or the benefits to the 
employees under this scheme are reduced in any manner, the 
exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

10. Where for any reason the employer fails to pay the 
premium etc. within the due date, as fixed by the Life Insu¬ 
rance Corporation of India, and the policy is allowed to 
lapse, the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled, 

11. In case of default, if any made by the employer in 
payment of premium the responsibility for payment of 
assurance benefits to the nominee(s) /legal heir(s) of deceased 
member who would have been covered under the said scheme 
but for grant of this exemption, shall be that of the employer. 

12. Upon the death of the member covered under the 
Group Insurance Scheme this Life Insurance Corporation of 
India shall ensure prompt payment of the sum assured to 
the nominccts) /Legal heir(s) of the deceased member 
entitled for it and in any case within one month from the 
receipt of claims complete in all respects. 

B. N. SOM, 

Central Provident Fund Commissioner

surance Corporation of India, and the policy is allowed to 
lapse the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

11. In case of default, if any made by the employer ip 
payment of premium the responsibility for payment of as¬ 
surance benefits to the nominee(s)/legal heir(s) of deceased 
member who would have been covered under the said 
scheme but for grant of this exemption, Shall be that of the 
employer. 

12. Upon the death of the member covered under the 
Group Insurance Scheme the Life Insurance Corporation of 
India shall ensure prompt payment of the sura assured to 
the nominee(s)/legal heir(s) of the deceased member en¬ 
titled for it and in any case within one month from the 
receipt of claims complete in all respects. 

B, N, SOM, 

Central Provident Fund Commissioner

Part III— Sec. 4] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, 
SCHEDULE-11 

1. Ttie employer in relation to each of the said establish¬ 
ment (hereinafter referred lo as the said employer) shall submit 
such returns to the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner 
concerned and maintain such accounts and provide such faci¬ 
lities for inspection, as the Central Provident Fund Commis¬ 
sioner may direct from time to time. 

2. The employer shall pay such Inspection charge* as the 
Central Government may, from time to time, direct 
clause (a) of sub-Section (2A) of Section. 17 of the said Act. 
within 15 days from the close of every month. 

3. All expenses Involved in the ad min istration of the Group 
Insurance Scheme, including maintenance of accounts, sub¬ 
mission of returns, payment if insurance premia, transfer 
of accounts, payment of inspection charges etc, shall be borne 
by the employer, 

4. The employer shall display on the Notice Board of the 
establishment, a copy of the rules of the Group Insurance 
Scheme as approved by the Central Government/Central 
Provident Fund Commissioner as and when amended, along- 
with translation of the salient features thereof in the language 
of majority of the employees. 

5. Whereas an employee, who is already a member of the 
Employees’ Provident Fund or the Provident Fund of an 
establishment exempted under the said Act, is employed in 
hia establishment the employer shall immediately admit him 
as a member of the Group Insurance Scheme and pay neces¬ 
sary Premium In respect of him to the Life Insurance Corpo¬ 
ration of India. 

6 . The employer shall arrange to enhance the benefits 
available to the employees under the Group Insurance 
Scheme appropriately if the benefits available to the emp¬ 
loyees under the said Schomo are enhanced so that the bene¬ 
fits available under the Group Insurance Scheme are more 
favourable to the employees than the benefits admissible 
under the said Scheme. 

7. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Group 
Insurance Scheme, if on the death of an employee the 


JUNE 18, 1994 (JYAISTHA 28, 1916) 3221 

amounts payable under the scheme be less than the amount 
that wpuld be payable had the employee been covered tender 
the said Scheme, the empoyer shall pay the difference to 
'he nominee(s)/legal heir(s) of the employee as compensa¬ 
tion, 

S. No amendment ot the provisions of the Gioup Insur¬ 
ance Scheme shall be made without the prior approval of 
the Regional Provident Fund Commissioner concerned and 
where any amendment is likely to effect adversely the inte¬ 
rest of the employees his approval, give a reasonable 
opportunity to the employees to explain their point of view. 

9. Where for any reason, the employees of the said estab¬ 
lishment do not remain covered under the Group Insurance 
Scheme of the Life Insurance Corporation of India as al¬ 
ready adopted by the said establishment or the benefits to 
the employees under this scheme are reduced in any manner 
the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

10. Where for any reason, the employer fails to pay the 
premium etc. within the due date, as fixed by the Life In¬ 
surance Corporation of India, and the policy is allowed to 
lapse the exemption shall be liable to be cancelled. 

11. In case of default, if any made by the employer in 
payment of premium the responsibility for payment of as¬ 
surance benefits to the nomineefs) /legal heir(s) of deceased 
member who would have been covered under the said 
scheme but for grant of this exemption, shall be that of the 
employer. 

12. Upon the death of the member covered under the 
Oroup Insurance Scheme this Life Insurance Corporation of 
India shall ensure prompt payment of the sum assured to 
the nominee(s) /legal heir(s) of the deceased member en¬ 
titled for it and in any case within one month from the 
receipt of claims complete in all respect. 

B. N. SOM, 

Central Provident Fund Commissioner
